##### What was that? Meta and Google lose the social media addiction trial, Instagram allows carousel reorder, TikTok promises "hard work" to advertisers, YouTube's AI titles, and the rest is...

Sorry for the delay, which was caused by me falling asleep. If you’re new or unaware, I usually write this newsletter in one sitting on Sunday, and very rarely do I have to travel on Sunday, but yesterday was that day. So when I got home, I was way too tired to finish the newsletter...hope you don’t mind. If you do, my bad lol, happy Monday!

---

##### “Social Media Addiction” vs. “Free Speech” — Why?

My timeline went from people celebrating this huge win against Meta and Google to a bunch of people posting screenshots of their screen time and saying, “Oh, can I also sue?” to everyone panicking about free speech after media reports suggested how this landmark trial could [become](https://substack.com/redirect/b69f7bef-5e6c-4c5b-bedc-f4680e5bb1c5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) the basis for [more regulation](https://substack.com/redirect/dada02a7-7068-41bc-a6f4-1ef6f5f9470c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) and changes to how the internet works, not just for Meta and Google.

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!EtyT!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fdbe937c1-409e-4d34-8dd6-e44d5e582a3a_3408x2556.jpeg)](https://substack.com/redirect/9c878911-5ef3-45a0-ae40-059d0ad6f566?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)Everyone has got something wrong. And the only right thing is not being discussed enough: the argument of “free speech” in the context of social media addiction or harm is similar to the anti-far-right arguments being made by politicians across the globe. The “protect our democracy” brand platform is weak as f\*\*k, because the majority of people across the world are far more concerned with their financial security, and that is true in every country. For millions of marginalized people, the promise of democracy has never truly existed in the way it has been promised.

In a similar vein, most people do not care about free speech¹ because, first, you have to remember that the average social media user consumes more than they post. The internet is not that social anymore. Even if they do care about free speech, more users today feel negative about the world after scrolling through social media than positive. For more than a decade, the evidence against social media has been piling up. Self-harm cases have increased, and every year an internal research report leaks showing these platforms are [aware of the harm](https://substack.com/redirect/6cff1b35-e975-4d86-8227-ce38d1edb79a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) they cause.

Yes, there were various political organizations backing this lawsuit with the intent to regulate speech, not protect kids. But Meta and Google lost because they made statements like this: “[Harms to children, such as sexual exploitation and detriments to mental health, were inevitable on the company’s platforms due to their vast user bases](https://substack.com/redirect/185e6cd2-34e2-4856-940e-2249545d9056?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).” For years, they’ve been promising that their algorithms are now more personalized, cutting funding to their own oversight boards, and making a million other announcements like these.

How are they meant to be protected under the premise of free speech and [Section 230](https://substack.com/redirect/1a9f044d-7240-4ee6-adb2-65c909b210aa?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) when they are increasingly acting like they want to control everything that goes on in their apps and regularly neglect user feedback?

> The TL;DR is, New Mexico did not win a lawsuit, Meta and Google [lost it](https://substack.com/redirect/ba381291-d728-4737-84f8-0080aa70d38a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) through their own mistakes.²

---

It is a very complex issue. I will talk about it later in more detail in a dedicated newsletter about social media. For now, age verification and social media bans are half-baked solutions, and Section 230 is still important to protect the internet. But the “free speech” argument is very weak, especially in a global context and in the era of Trump 2.0.

**Fear sells, security buys.** “Democracy or free speech under threat” as a platform does sell, but economic and health concerns make people buy. Social media platforms are like politicians, everything is an agenda to them. If you and I want to protect our speech and access on social media, we have to assure the public of a better solution as much as we warn them about a worse scenario like the death of free speech.

* [iHeartMedia](https://substack.com/redirect/2edc1446-0518-4ac0-bd03-4e0602e8edbc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) and TikTok develop a new blueprint for artist launches’ with a campaign for Bruno Mars’ first solo album.

	+ TikTok, iHeartMedia team up for ‘[Romantic Radio](https://substack.com/redirect/be291137-332e-4e59-95a1-64a533e289df?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).’
* Meet the [TikTok realtors](https://substack.com/redirect/ede82184-7543-49a2-8ec5-acc7b5f054bb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) selling £20mn mansions on their socials.
* TikTok and FIFA reach [first-of-its-kind Preferred Platform](https://substack.com/redirect/5429b09c-8d31-4430-a715-2b1508152cc1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) agreement to enhance coverage of FIFA World Cup 2026.

* TikTok execs promise [advertisers](https://substack.com/redirect/b47741be-c6e2-4de9-b77c-1c6d738fb23e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) they’ve “[done the hard work](https://substack.com/redirect/3c5732b2-188c-4807-8d53-2c42dbc15bb4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” to earn trust since joint venture launch.

* [K-pop stars](https://substack.com/redirect/385cbd33-2445-4571-a780-c58d6f533932?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), actors flock to TikTok LIVE for direct monetization.
* TikTok is testing [a redesigned profile UI](https://substack.com/redirect/11f5f140-ea9d-4a1f-9c3d-322a78b5a922?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), it’s somewhat similar to Threads UI.
* Inside the [TikTok influencer wars](https://substack.com/redirect/a353fad6-d4a4-485e-8f44-9c4c37d08b51?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) that show just how divided London really is.
* TikTok for Business accounts [targeted](https://substack.com/redirect/a4ec3bee-068a-497c-98b1-7eaf04d4a891?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) in phishing campaign.

* Meta updates its [Threads Marketing API](https://substack.com/redirect/5fa72608-f5ca-420b-9cda-1b50cd01b36e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Instagram working on [an AI label for profiles](https://substack.com/redirect/3628218c-7cd3-424d-b45f-33fd0ac1365b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) that primarily post AI-generated or modified content.
* Threads tests “[Pick up where you left off](https://substack.com/redirect/12e73e8a-d54c-4af0-97c1-d5e2ef3cb7d6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” feature, allowing users to revisit old content.
* Instagram adds [AI transition option](https://substack.com/redirect/f79991fd-14da-4f4d-aff5-d930ba581947?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for still image-based Stories
* [Meta Business Suite](https://substack.com/redirect/6bf0e28e-9d2a-422a-84f0-5a6017438963?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to support collaboration invites for Instagram Reels.

	+ Meta’s [AI makeover](https://substack.com/redirect/87d89093-89ed-4859-9afe-e01800fc71fa?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) starts at the top.
	+ [Mark Zuckerberg](https://substack.com/redirect/5b177566-fb57-4510-b04f-1838d5d0ab00?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is building an AI Agent to help him be CEO.
	+ Hugo Barra’s [return to Meta 5 years](https://substack.com/redirect/ba7fac61-885c-473d-b200-453fa1931b2e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) after exit underscores Zuckerberg’s AI urgency.
	+ U.S. needs “[whole new workforce](https://substack.com/redirect/304a4465-bb37-44f4-9500-a0e678dc7533?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” for AI, Meta president says.
* [WhatsApp](https://substack.com/redirect/7fb227d2-d111-4411-8030-cda5bf397cf2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) adds new features to simplify storage, switch accounts, and more.
	+ Meta hit with consumer lawsuit over access to [WhatsApp messages](https://substack.com/redirect/d3cbe391-42c1-4f43-9687-2b05a3248ffd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
	+ WhatsApp can now [draft AI-generated responses](https://substack.com/redirect/e4055807-775c-4e06-bf87-100600b41860?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) based on your conversations.
* Meta told to pay [$375m for misleading users](https://substack.com/redirect/5c96ea00-d0be-458c-a0a8-53653182bee3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) over child safety.
* Meta boosts [Texas AI data center](https://substack.com/redirect/769e1079-ccd2-4b29-aa6c-d7c2556a000f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) investment to $10 billion.
	+ Meta partners with [Arm](https://substack.com/redirect/0f1c8d85-8847-420e-9d4c-cffc3042a8e3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to develop new class of data center silicon.
* ‘Meta [suspended our accounts](https://substack.com/redirect/eaac6c64-84b8-4df4-91bf-1f8936e113c0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) and we don’t know why.’
	+ China bars executives at [Meta-owned AI company](https://substack.com/redirect/5bb5bb8d-5d97-439c-ad98-3af43979cb80?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) from leaving country.
* Meta to launch [new AI Glasses](https://substack.com/redirect/5386ac08-fe86-4737-a3b2-4d8d5e42853d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) aimed at prescription wearers.
* Meta [tests](https://substack.com/redirect/c2b9fa3f-8a1a-4ae8-8ec9-991b1a806482?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Avocado 9B, Avocado Mango Agent, and more.
* Meta’s Oversight Board warns that “[Community Notes](https://substack.com/redirect/d874edd7-7eda-491b-ba98-42b6354f71ea?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” aren’t a proper substitute for fact-checking globally.
* WhatsApp to allows users to [create custom audiences](https://substack.com/redirect/c6370e0e-0e7e-4377-8ed5-824a971497ef?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for status updates.

* YouTube adds [Top Sports Podcast lineup](https://substack.com/redirect/85e77944-df68-4b3a-976a-24e113e447cf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for advertisers.
* ‘[Jeopardy!](https://substack.com/redirect/356fb4ab-2a44-4bdd-a304-f40d98d5f5f8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ to launch YouTube edition: First contestants are Monét X Change, Rebecca Black and Brennan Lee Mulligan.

* [YouTube exec](https://substack.com/redirect/d46aaf7b-c2fe-4e0f-b844-e110dbe42421?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) counters Netflix rivalry talk, highlighting platform’s global reach and $100 billion in creator economy payout.
* Symphonic [acquires](https://substack.com/redirect/6c4ec9f7-176d-47e4-8e21-503c030bdecd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) music distribution and YouTube monetization platform Distro Nation.
* YouTube TV’s [$65 Sports Plan](https://substack.com/redirect/1ab71f21-a235-4345-81a8-c2442075bc6f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) just got even cheaper.
* “[Educational](https://substack.com/redirect/f8978ed7-0913-4c08-9ae1-07914385bcff?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” AI YouTube videos accused of teaching kids to play in traffic & eat toxic food.
* The YouTube app’s [Cast button](https://substack.com/redirect/c1b97611-df38-4124-9bbe-bdd4428d035f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) has vanished for some users.

* [Kroger’s new Google partnership](https://substack.com/redirect/53c1da35-0c40-46ba-8421-8a2f610bc75c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) lets advertisers target precise retail audiences on YouTube.
	+ [Roku](https://substack.com/redirect/24277dfd-0e19-48ed-93ce-06e904a5b7bb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) announced as partner for Google’s new Confidential Publisher Match solution.
* Google [released](https://substack.com/redirect/bb558e19-42db-4592-b22b-9a4eaf679692?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) v23.2 of the Google Ads API.

* Google-Agent user agent [identifies](https://substack.com/redirect/1156ed08-68c8-4c53-bd5f-446e160dc942?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) AI agent traffic in server logs.
* Google responds to error that causes old branding to persist in [SERPs](https://substack.com/redirect/94f85a49-9583-4d99-a530-153cf8a3df14?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Google is launching [Search Live globally](https://substack.com/redirect/b8fd1112-05aa-4f0c-9837-d6afacd8bed7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Google adds [seasonal creative theming](https://substack.com/redirect/7f3ecab8-8894-433b-895e-601dcbecac75?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to PMax asset groups.
* Google is tightening [political content rules](https://substack.com/redirect/fb52fc47-4111-42d2-8bac-26ea004e1cdb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for Shopping ads starting April 16.
* Google integrates Gemini Advantage into [Display & Video 360](https://substack.com/redirect/95e30cc0-1a65-4053-9a22-6a79f4cf46f6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g).
* Gemini could let you [transfer chat history](https://substack.com/redirect/7dab0d9c-111b-41d0-8af7-04f990a9a205?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) from other AI apps, like a game of ‘telephone.’

[![](https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!J7DY!,w_1100,c_limit,f_auto,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Facd06a12-7508-4bfa-91b1-8c434a20fc84_1100x220.png)](https://substack.com/redirect/f3881337-068e-4dc7-8c10-2c8e8f97f72a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)* X dangles [$200K for advertisers](https://substack.com/redirect/25645239-28fd-4b55-afb8-5fce5e4d1b15?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to return to the platform, leaked deck shows.
* Elon Musk’s X [restructures](https://substack.com/redirect/27438b20-8ed7-4152-93fa-9c9064203e9c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) ahead of SpaceX IPO.
* X moves the [ashes of TweetDeck](https://substack.com/redirect/36c26074-a069-4501-8000-83a438b4f109?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) behind its $40 Premium+ subscription.
* Elon Musk [should let](https://substack.com/redirect/3596e5a4-62f0-44a7-a919-563d98ca5028?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) Nikita Bier fix [revenue sharing](https://substack.com/redirect/c5ef11aa-d710-4fc5-9b4e-4dcfff0e3abd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on X.
* X lays off [CMO](https://substack.com/redirect/8face458-2d39-4dd2-97a0-9cfbefb71a95?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), other staff ahead of IPO.
* Musk’s xAI ‘[doubling down](https://substack.com/redirect/f1f34b5a-8800-4160-9514-7fd5b62a703d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ on AI videos after OpenAI nixes Sora.
* X’s [Grok-powered algorithm](https://substack.com/redirect/f9bdb8eb-6d0b-4b42-a3fb-8c7be20f0463?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) launches next week.
* xAI prepares [Skills support](https://substack.com/redirect/498a5143-c40e-420c-9214-8b46cf8ac0a4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) for Grok to rival Claude and ChatGPT.
* White House turns down [Elon Musk’s offer](https://substack.com/redirect/b82a59fd-a857-469d-a9a6-c2f20c17bccb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to pay TSA workers during DHS shutdown.
* Musk says [Delaware judge](https://substack.com/redirect/18a36ab1-9e32-48e6-a2e3-90597201ad6a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) showed bias by backing his adversaries on LinkedIn.
* [X Pro](https://substack.com/redirect/5d514687-0525-4b22-b90c-74bdb85b9654?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) is now limited to only Premium+ subscribers.
* [Grok Imagine](https://substack.com/redirect/c3662522-8055-458a-b248-89ad1a215ff8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) now locked behind Premium subscription, days after Ask Grok was restricted for free users.
* X will not mark content from [Adobe Photoshop](https://substack.com/redirect/c0b49479-cdf4-4a59-a4eb-8668badbccf4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) as AI generated content.
	+ A first look at the “[History](https://substack.com/redirect/6b4e8bd2-922e-4388-9531-fe6ffcee2bde?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)” page coming to X for iOS.
* Dutch court [bans Grok](https://substack.com/redirect/8cef641e-26ee-4a9f-8771-7d8b7d251744?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) from generating fake nudes, threatens €100K daily penalties.

* Reddit prods judge to move [Anthropic case back](https://substack.com/redirect/54207a6e-347d-45c3-8d17-7799b6e6d329?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to state court.
	+ [Billionaire Reddit CEO Steve Huffman](https://substack.com/redirect/21a52f66-a437-4bc4-a3c2-3e9300ebf650?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) says his company will ‘go heavy’ on hiring graduates because ‘they’re so much more AI native’ than older peers.
* AV1’s open, royalty-free promise in question as [Dolby sues Snapchat](https://substack.com/redirect/2c7bd9ac-1eec-4706-9688-20ad230cc881?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) over codec.

	+ Snapchat hops onto the “full-funnel” bandwagon with [ad formats](https://substack.com/redirect/7a78e3dd-5cb2-4a39-8b8d-ba69ec982db6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) that command attention.

	+ Samsung taps [Amazon DSP interactive ad tech](https://substack.com/redirect/3c680399-401c-4a46-a811-cbf802c043e1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), pitches full-funnel CTV at NewFronts.
* MiQ acquires [Adsmovil](https://substack.com/redirect/363adefa-44c3-44f4-9f1c-0cb059193ee9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), Creating Largest Independent Programmatic Offering.
* Page view slump [continues](https://substack.com/redirect/4762a5c3-9eca-4b98-8570-df78dabc92a9?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) across regional newsbrands.
* [Martin Sorrell’s outlook](https://substack.com/redirect/16a7b966-76e7-40c3-8d6b-9d7bc38b3731?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) on AI, media transparency and agency upheaval.
* [Uproxx](https://substack.com/redirect/7e7be031-404b-4e97-97a9-b0f490b91bfb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) makes play for TV ad spend with TikTok-ready shows.
* [GoWit](https://substack.com/redirect/1d0cf580-7394-4657-83a3-bd9076e829fb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) and Futura DDB team up to transform retail media in the Adriatic
* Bertelsmann signals [M&A appetite](https://substack.com/redirect/9e1951e5-d84d-45ef-aca7-ad3c13ff20b7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) after RTL weakness drags earnings.
* Government accused of ‘[moving the goalposts](https://substack.com/redirect/073f7a0e-9db6-400a-9c68-c919eb40dbe8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g)’ following latest LHF ad ban consultation.
* ZeroToOne.AI acquires [GroundTruth](https://substack.com/redirect/2f07739d-4be8-4db9-97b2-b1d19d612d4f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g), an advertising and measurement platform to scale predictive intelligence.
* Rakuten Advertising & [Similarweb](https://substack.com/redirect/936bfcf4-a2c0-404a-ad08-a06e92deb860?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) partner on LLM visibility features.
* [Xumo](https://substack.com/redirect/2f1d01ad-d734-4e83-a7b6-4a75f4e57eb1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) positions unified streaming ad approach at NewFronts.
* [Ofcom](https://substack.com/redirect/2d042015-9607-4e4d-99a0-59c4dd32b1f6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to investigate complaints of climate change denial for first time since 2017.
* DoubleVerify partners with [Spectrum Reach](https://substack.com/redirect/c5c8795c-b79f-46c5-a846-2faa9181b5f0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI_zv_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g) to improve streaming TV campaign performance.

<div dir="auto" style="--image-offset-margin: -120px;padding: 32px 0 0 0;font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><div dir="auto" style="text-align: initial;font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;width: 100%;word-break: break-word;margin-bottom: 16px;font-family: 'SF Pro Display', -apple-system, system-ui, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Inter', 'Segoe UI', Roboto, Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif, 'Apple Color Emoji', 'Segoe UI Emoji', 'Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: 400;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;margin-top: 0;">Sorry for the delay, which was caused by me falling asleep. If you’re new or unaware, I usually write this newsletter in one sitting on Sunday, and very rarely do I have to travel on Sunday, but yesterday was that day. So when I got home, I was way too tired to finish the newsletter...hope you don’t mind. If you do, my bad lol, happy Monday!</p>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Before you go further, Attention is like money – you can waste it or invest it. If you invest it in my paid newsletter, you'll get dividends for years.</p></blockquote>

</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Instagram now finally lets users </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/afadec97-4601-4738-9989-a2dcec18010b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">reorder carousel</a><span> images and videos after </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/4e9f20ea-65d9-444d-bd53-5bec437926e6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">publishing</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e1b21d41-92fd-46ad-9f72-9bdc7b13d371?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">TikTok</a><span> pitches advertisers at NewFronts </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7dc2ff4d-423f-494a-8074-443a072ed44d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">a year after</a><span> its near-ban and sale.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a50423df-65a9-4181-a597-6ee617278271?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Jury</a><span> finds Meta and Google negligent in </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e4d54b88-6778-4309-839a-f190e42bbb9d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">social media harms trial</a><span>.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Do TikTok, Instagram, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/bacc0f7c-1c8c-4e05-8435-86230e7f8086?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">social media bans for teens</a><span> really work?</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Judge </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/dc06184d-ce50-48cb-89dd-f3308c323884?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">dismisses</a><span> lawsuit by Musk’s X Corp accusing </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/49e8508e-a104-485f-81d0-9703d2ad2e74?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">advertisers</a><span> of illegal boycott.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f78f31a0-e8f9-4d9e-9e65-1d025684760d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">YouTube</a><span> is building infrastructure for the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2c3a02f3-6b88-4fd2-b1c8-238176a90b94?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">full creator-brand partnership life</a><span> cycle.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Reddit will require “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/eb1f26ef-69d3-4d7e-9d51-77e42cc6526a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">fishy</a><span>” accounts to verify they are </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/41cd32de-1a25-4aaf-b8c5-b716abf110b8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">run by a human</a><span>.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: rgb(0,0,0,.1);border: none;"/></div>

<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="727"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b61301bb-9573-4984-96f0-6ebc2ca650e1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/484d6c9a-ab2b-4a12-b322-17f701d13b66\_1100x220.png","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":220,"width":1100,"resizeWidth":727,"bytes":48232,"alt":null,"title":null,"type":"image/png","href":null,"belowTheFold":false,"topImage":false,"internalRedirect":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/i/163845782?img=https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F484d6c9a-ab2b-4a12-b322-17f701d13b66\_1100x220.png","isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="110" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!zM4F!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F484d6c9a-ab2b-4a12-b322-17f701d13b66\_1100x220.png" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" width="550"/></a></td>

</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Reddit introduces </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/13ba77ba-26ed-4563-b457-717e33c78dd7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Collection Ads</a><span> in latest shopping push to attract </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ebad8d90-ef0e-4d43-af4c-2bcbc35fd586?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">retail brands</a><span>.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/398d4216-3380-4e52-b68c-4245bfe58694?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Pinterest</a><span> launches a new feature to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/53524ae7-89ad-4576-a06f-47cecfbc5bae?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">promote a Pin</a><span>.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/abb7c842-eb9e-467f-b27e-5470d1e5639d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">OpenAI</a><span> enters its focus era by killing </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0d064083-9305-4a17-bba6-ca7f8b10f4c3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Sora</a><span>. The decision is all about </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5f434860-3bb4-4772-8d04-2e78048dafe1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI compute</a><span>.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/910e8fb7-8ff6-4f2f-b7e9-ebe2631a6708?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Disney</a><span> says it will find ways to</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b18c9330-67be-463f-acc0-1edb49f30086?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> peddle slop elsewhere</a><span> after pulling out of OpenAI deal.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The Oscars aren’t just </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1a49870a-ea12-41fe-9d0c-642d119996b5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">moving to YouTube</a><span>—They’re also leaving their longtime LA home.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Why Netflix </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0a9ae079-4ea2-4aee-bb1c-2943ee694085?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">hiked</a><span> prices, explained in </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5880da65-2e66-4a63-aaea-0d6b95279e28?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">one chart</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ad3b129f-ced3-4974-a88f-e73095f5d349?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Roblox</a><span> to overhaul ad policies, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2de59456-ed21-421d-9f0b-e0e77656048a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">introduce</a><span> revenue sharing in 2027.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>My week of eating </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/efd55f15-4e2c-416e-9b6b-e0e0afc605f1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">literal AI Slop.</a></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1e6c2d8d-7291-428b-8f98-cfd8d036ae55?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Bernie Sanders</a><span> had a long conversation with Claude AI. Reddit </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1cc85635-7249-4144-ba9a-3b9c56ab3f74?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">didn’t hold</a><span> back.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Why can’t </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e69f9c6d-7b1c-4441-9bb0-330bbcc990df?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">TikTok identify AI generated ads</a><span> when I can?</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6a167227-d6c2-49df-9cf7-6415a5d45d62?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Texas lawyer and part-time pastor</a><span> who beat Meta and Google.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7a49442e-3aff-4ee9-b7cb-2f92488c8240?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Young guys on TikTok</a><span> are obsessed with fertility and “spermmaxxing”.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e44d2140-a0d3-4d41-8905-f2df1e5fdff1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">EU</a><span> probes </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f21b35ba-ba9d-4ba2-ae31-c65f440033b0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Snapchat for failing to protect kids</a><span> from grooming and illegal goods.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b16ee7a2-93af-4ae0-a531-7a93aa97ac71?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Snapchat</a><span> launches AI clips format in </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/37298ede-17f6-41c8-9f2c-063c9a55b245?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Lens Studio.</a></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>12 tons of KitKat bars </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/240a4c88-5e86-4c64-b34c-245761dc527d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">stolen in chocolaty heist</a><span> in Europe, </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b104899b-7fb4-453a-88ef-ab08ce215719?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Nestle</a><span> says.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: rgb(0,0,0,.1);border: none;"/></div>
<h3 style="position: relative;font-family: 'SF Pro Display',-apple-system-headline,system-ui,-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif,'Apple Color Emoji','Segoe UI Emoji','Segoe UI Symbol';font-weight: bold;-webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;-moz-osx-font-smoothing: antialiased;-webkit-appearance: optimizelegibility;-moz-appearance: optimizelegibility;appearance: optimizelegibility;margin: 1em 0 0.625em 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 1.16em;font-size: 1.375em;">“Social Media Addiction” vs. “Free Speech” — Why?</h3>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>My timeline went from people celebrating this huge win against Meta and Google to a bunch of people posting screenshots of their screen time and saying, “Oh, can I also sue?” to everyone panicking about free speech after media reports suggested how this landmark trial could </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b69f7bef-5e6c-4c5b-bedc-f4680e5bb1c5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">become</a><span> the basis for </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/dada02a7-7068-41bc-a6f4-1ef6f5f9470c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">more regulation</a><span> and changes to how the internet works, not just for Meta and Google.</span></p>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
<td align="left" style="text-align: center;" width="613"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9c878911-5ef3-45a0-ae40-059d0ad6f566?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="position: relative;flex-direction: column;align-items: center;padding: 0;width: auto;height: auto;border: none;text-decoration: none;display: block;margin: 0;" target="\_blank"><img alt="" data-attrs='{"src":"https://substack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com/public/images/dbe937c1-409e-4d34-8dd6-e44d5e582a3a\_3408x2556.jpeg","srcNoWatermark":null,"fullscreen":null,"imageSize":null,"height":1092,"width":1456,"resizeWidth":613,"bytes":482942,"alt":null,"title":null,"type":"image/jpeg","href":null,"belowTheFold":true,"topImage":false,"internalRedirect":"https://thesocialjuice.substack.com/i/192516129?img=https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fdbe937c1-409e-4d34-8dd6-e44d5e582a3a\_3408x2556.jpeg","isProcessing":false,"align":null,"offset":false}' height="412.5" src="https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/%24s\_!EtyT!,w\_1100,c\_limit,f\_auto,q\_auto:good,fl\_progressive:steep/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fdbe937c1-409e-4d34-8dd6-e44d5e582a3a\_3408x2556.jpeg" style="border: none !important;vertical-align: middle;display: block;-ms-interpolation-mode: bicubic;height: auto;margin-bottom: 0;width: auto !important;max-width: 100% !important;margin: 0 auto;" width="550"/></a></td>
<td style="text-align: center;"></td>
</tr></tbody></table></figure></div>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">Everyone has got something wrong. And the only right thing is not being discussed enough: the argument of “free speech” in the context of social media addiction or harm is similar to the anti-far-right arguments being made by politicians across the globe. The “protect our democracy” brand platform is weak as f\*\*k, because the majority of people across the world are far more concerned with their financial security, and that is true in every country. For millions of marginalized people, the promise of democracy has never truly existed in the way it has been promised.</p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>In a similar vein, most people do not care about free speech</span><span data-component-name="FootnoteAnchorToDOMStatic" style="font-size: 20px;">¹</span><span> because, first, you have to remember that the average social media user consumes more than they post. The internet is not that social anymore. Even if they do care about free speech, more users today feel negative about the world after scrolling through social media than positive. For more than a decade, the evidence against social media has been piling up. Self-harm cases have increased, and every year an internal research report leaks showing these platforms are </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6cff1b35-e975-4d86-8227-ce38d1edb79a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">aware of the harm</a><span> they cause.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Yes, there were various political organizations backing this lawsuit with the intent to regulate speech, not protect kids. But Meta and Google lost because they made statements like this: “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/185e6cd2-34e2-4856-940e-2249545d9056?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Harms to children, such as sexual exploitation and detriments to mental health, were inevitable on the company’s platforms due to their vast user bases</a><span>.” For years, they’ve been promising that their algorithms are now more personalized, cutting funding to their own oversight boards, and making a million other announcements like these.</span></p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>How are they meant to be protected under the premise of free speech and </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1a9f044d-7240-4ee6-adb2-65c909b210aa?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Section 230</a><span> when they are increasingly acting like they want to control everything that goes on in their apps and regularly neglect user feedback?</span></p>
<blockquote style="border-left: 4px solid #e85c57;margin: 20px 0;padding: 0;"><p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);margin-left: 20px;line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><span>The TL;DR is, New Mexico did not win a lawsuit, Meta and Google </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ba381291-d728-4737-84f8-0080aa70d38a?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">lost it</a><span> through their own mistakes.</span><span data-component-name="FootnoteAnchorToDOMStatic" style="font-size: 20px;">²</span></p></blockquote>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: rgb(0,0,0,.1);border: none;"/></div>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;">It is a very complex issue. I will talk about it later in more detail in a dedicated newsletter about social media. For now, age verification and social media bans are half-baked solutions, and Section 230 is still important to protect the internet. But the “free speech” argument is very weak, especially in a global context and in the era of Trump 2.0.</p>
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;font-size: 16px;"><strong>Fear sells, security buys. </strong><span>“Democracy or free speech under threat” as a platform does sell, but economic and health concerns make people buy. Social media platforms are like politicians, everything is an agenda to them. If you and I want to protect our speech and access on social media, we have to assure the public of a better solution as much as we warn them about a worse scenario like the death of free speech.</span></p>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;"><hr style="margin: 32px 0;padding: 0;height: 1px;background: rgb(0,0,0,.1);border: none;"/></div>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a35a3ce4-cac8-4ed0-8586-082cbfe297e0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">OpenAI</a><span> puts erotic chatbot plans on hold ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3fde4c94-668b-4b31-ad2c-5df55886bf55?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">indefinitely</a><span>.’</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1db60c9d-aa8c-4068-967c-9616f191b63b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Stanford</a><span>: Folk are </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3e22e8ea-37b7-487b-8e06-c1511125ded1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">getting dangerously attached</a><span> to AI that always tells them they’re right.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Netflix’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b4149399-245e-4558-87b1-cee8f4a7863c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">MLB debut</a><span> criticized for missing ABS challenge, too much </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/82c7d8e2-262e-4fe1-86e0-d3467e2ddc7c?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">self-promotion</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Wikipedia </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/08a02a2b-c061-416c-bcac-7743ff50777f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">bans</a><span> AI-generated content.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>EU backs </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c08baaf0-f5a1-43a4-9fc6-ef9fa9ca91bf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">nude app ban</a><span> and delays to landmark AI rules.</span></p></li>

<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a2765125-d0b1-4b98-b6bf-a3921c7322ad?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">veteran podcasters</a><span> hanging up their headphones.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>California bill would require </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c2b149fc-0aa7-4a58-938e-04074b9b5f31?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">parent bloggers to delete content</a><span> of minors on social media.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The real March Madness is </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0c8581a4-1144-4727-b949-22ad1a819bb7?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">the corporate race</a><span> to ink viral stars.</span></p></li>
</ul>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>TikTok is </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0599b3ad-650c-4f5f-9223-f6339d7bdfdb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">casting actors to make its own micro dramas</a><span> as it jumps headfirst into the hot format.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>TikTok For Business launches “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/41de7ddc-b972-40b6-83ba-14c45523b3be?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Watch it. Love it. Want it.</a><span>” brand platform and OOH campaign.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/92b86ca5-4f31-46cb-bbc9-55be2d7f01de?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">TikTok</a><span> returns to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e120378e-8656-482d-a137-0bd76bbb02d3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Juno Awards</a><span> after Canada reverses shutdown order over national security.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2edc1446-0518-4ac0-bd03-4e0602e8edbc?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">iHeartMedia</a><span> and TikTok develop a new blueprint for artist launches’ with a campaign for Bruno Mars’ first solo album.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>TikTok announces </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/13744061-e46d-4478-9086-550bdcd5f35e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Charlie Puth</a><span> as the latest guest on ‘TikTok In The Mix’, sponsored by T-Mobile.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>TikTok, iHeartMedia team up for ‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/be291137-332e-4e59-95a1-64a533e289df?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Romantic Radio</a><span>.’</span></p></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meet the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ede82184-7543-49a2-8ec5-acc7b5f054bb?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">TikTok realtors</a><span> selling £20mn mansions on their socials.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>TikTok and FIFA reach </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5429b09c-8d31-4430-a715-2b1508152cc1?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">first-of-its-kind Preferred Platform</a><span> agreement to enhance coverage of FIFA World Cup 2026.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>French education ministry reports </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/604ce55d-6b09-444b-89d9-dccac5ba656f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">TikTok</a><span> to Paris prosecutor.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>TikTok execs promise </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/b47741be-c6e2-4de9-b77c-1c6d738fb23e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">advertisers</a><span> they’ve “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3c5732b2-188c-4807-8d53-2c42dbc15bb4?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">done the hard work</a><span>” to earn trust since joint venture launch.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>The new </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c982b8d2-a6fb-4df5-a184-f8a2d0982111?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">“Logo Takeover” ad format</a><span> allows companies to co-brand with TikTok in a way that captures users’ attention.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>TikTok courts CMOs with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1848f08f-962b-428e-bd91-c8508206b3ac?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">first-ever Collective</a><span>, as it targets bigger budgets.</span></p></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>TikTok Shop sellers gain easier access to their own </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9fed93ed-4855-4b5b-a5f1-4568c4b474d5?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">user-generated content</a><span>.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/94912b70-a30c-4f94-929a-4e379b8c8fbf?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Bazaarvoice</a><span> integrates with TikTok Shop to provide syndicated reviews.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>‘</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6a2754f1-058a-4bef-a1cc-7351e6ee392d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Kids say they take a quick look at TikTok</a><span>’: a new kind of distracted driving is on the rise.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/cb720fb0-0029-4715-bd5e-6ec5c7e5da63?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Angry Ginge </a><span>says under-14s should not have TikTok.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>MAC offers</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/9e5ebd97-c471-481f-8234-9516f079eff8?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> commission to UK staff</a><span> to sell products on TikTok live.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>ByteDance’s new AI video generation model, Dreamina Seedance 2.0, comes to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/bbea2256-1ddc-49fc-9b28-dae1d095e097?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">CapCut</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/385cbd33-2445-4571-a780-c58d6f533932?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">K-pop stars</a><span>, actors flock to TikTok LIVE for direct monetization.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>TikTok is testing </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/11f5f140-ea9d-4a1f-9c3d-322a78b5a922?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">a redesigned profile UI</a><span>, it’s somewhat similar to Threads UI. </span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Inside the </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a353fad6-d4a4-485e-8f44-9c4c37d08b51?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">TikTok influencer wars</a><span> that show just how divided London really is.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>TikTok for Business accounts </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/a4ec3bee-068a-497c-98b1-7eaf04d4a891?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">targeted</a><span> in phishing campaign.</span></p></li>
</ul>
<div style="font-size: 16px;line-height: 26px;margin: 32px auto;"><figure style="width: 100%;margin: 0 auto;"><table border="0" cellpadding="0" cellspacing="0" data-component-name="Image2ToDOMStatic" style="mso-padding-alt: 1em 0 1.6em;" width="100%"><tbody><tr>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2eb34883-5d8b-45a8-840b-d395a27c11cd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Meta</a><span> goes deeper into shopping, tests </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5eb2af01-301c-49a5-89ea-d264685ca69f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">creator commissions on Instagram</a><span> and new buy button within ads.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/2eb34883-5d8b-45a8-840b-d395a27c11cd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Expansion</a><span> of Reels trending ads, Creator Marketplace, and launch of AI features.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Instagram tests </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6d1efdb4-6717-4a9f-9289-037d5b863733?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">auto-download reels</a><span> for offline viewing feature.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/49ad90cf-62bd-47f2-8939-d838d950165d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Edits App</a><span> adds new AI style for fonts, transform tool, and new updates to links + template. </span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta updates its </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5fa72608-f5ca-420b-9cda-1b50cd01b36e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Threads Marketing API</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Instagram working on </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/3628218c-7cd3-424d-b45f-33fd0ac1365b?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">an AI label for profiles</a><span> that primarily post AI-generated or modified content.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Threads tests “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/12e73e8a-d54c-4af0-97c1-d5e2ef3cb7d6?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Pick up where you left off</a><span>” feature, allowing users to revisit old content.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Instagram adds </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f79991fd-14da-4f4d-aff5-d930ba581947?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI transition option</a><span> for still image-based Stories</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/6bf0e28e-9d2a-422a-84f0-5a6017438963?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Meta Business Suite</a><span> to support collaboration invites for Instagram Reels.</span></p></li>
</ul>

<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/1c4444ea-2cc8-4e37-a9fa-6bae3843a6b3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Meta</a><span> announces </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/f3218b3c-8275-4ba5-beaa-3744a7d54d35?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">new retail media tools</a><span> to grab more ad dollars and tests testing one-click checkout.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Zuckerberg launches </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ca036eb5-2a98-4f70-9280-762a4735f283?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Meta Small Business</a><span>, a company-wide priority to drive AI adoption.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/87d89093-89ed-4859-9afe-e01800fc71fa?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">AI makeover</a><span> starts at the top.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5b177566-fb57-4510-b04f-1838d5d0ab00?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Mark Zuckerberg</a><span> is building an AI Agent to help him be CEO.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Hugo Barra’s </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/ba7fac61-885c-473d-b200-453fa1931b2e?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">return to Meta 5 years</a><span> after exit underscores Zuckerberg’s AI urgency.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>U.S. needs “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/304a4465-bb37-44f4-9500-a0e678dc7533?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">whole new workforce</a><span>” for AI, Meta president says.</span></p></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/7fb227d2-d111-4411-8030-cda5bf397cf2?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">WhatsApp</a><span> adds new features to simplify storage, switch accounts, and more.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;">
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta hit with consumer lawsuit over access to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d3cbe391-42c1-4f43-9687-2b05a3248ffd?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">WhatsApp messages</a><span>.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>WhatsApp can now </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/e4055807-775c-4e06-bf87-100600b41860?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">draft AI-generated responses</a><span> based on your conversations.</span></p></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta told to pay</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5c96ea00-d0be-458c-a0a8-53653182bee3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;"> $375m for misleading users</a><span> over child safety.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>Meta boosts </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/769e1079-ccd2-4b29-aa6c-d7c2556a000f?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Texas AI data center</a><span> investment to $10 billion.</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta partners with </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/0f1c8d85-8847-420e-9d4c-cffc3042a8e3?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Arm</a><span> to develop new class of data center silicon.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;">
<p style="margin: 0 0 20px 0;color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;"><span>‘Meta </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/eaac6c64-84b8-4df4-91bf-1f8936e113c0?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">suspended our accounts</a><span> and we don’t know why.’</span></p>
<ul style="margin-top: 0;padding: 0;"><li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>China bars executives at </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5bb5bb8d-5d97-439c-ad98-3af43979cb80?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Meta-owned AI company</a><span> from leaving country.</span></p></li></ul>
</li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta to launch </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/5386ac08-fe86-4737-a3b2-4d8d5e42853d?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">new AI Glasses</a><span> aimed at prescription wearers.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c2b9fa3f-8a1a-4ae8-8ec9-991b1a806482?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">tests</a><span> Avocado 9B, Avocado Mango Agent, and more.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>Meta’s Oversight Board warns that “</span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/d874edd7-7eda-491b-ba98-42b6354f71ea?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">Community Notes</a><span>” aren’t a proper substitute for fact-checking globally.</span></p></li>
<li style="margin: 8px 0 0 32px;mso-special-format: bullet;"><p style="color: rgb(54,55,55);line-height: 26px;margin-bottom: 0;box-sizing: border-box;padding-left: 4px;font-size: 16px;margin: 0;"><span>WhatsApp to allows users to </span><a href="https://substack.com/redirect/c6370e0e-0e7e-4377-8ed5-824a971497ef?j=eyJ1IjoiNnE2ZWdsIn0.JeUaI03hkTZe3g0Y-WbKcpI\_zv\_M4N2A50pyRXMoP1g" rel="" style="color: #e85c57;text-decoration: none;">create custom audiences</a><span> for status updates.</span></p></li>
</ul>
